## Detailed Description

A class for complex polynomials.

A class of polynomials with complex coefficients. Ring operations are
overloaded. Need to be carefull about the degree of the result.

Definition at line [24](polynomial_8hpp_source.html#l00024) of file
[polynomial.hpp](polynomial_8hpp_source.html).

| ) | |   
  
construction with an array of coefficients

Warning

    c[d] has to be nonzero; i.e., d really has to be the degree of c 

Parameters

     d| a degree of the created polynomial   
---|---  
c| an array of coefficient where c[i] is the coef. of z^i

## ◆ coef

std::vector<COMPLEX> iRRAM::POLYNOMIAL::coef  
---  
  
coefficients

coefficients are stored as a std::vector<COMPLEX> coef. coef[0] is the
constant. coef[n] is the coefficient of x^n

## Detailed Description

A class for random function.

The instance of this class indicates the single sample path of wiener process
which is sample from Wiener distribution

Definition at line [21](wiener_8hpp_source.html#l00021) of file
[wiener.hpp](wiener_8hpp_source.html).

## Constructor & Destructor Documentation

## ◆ WIENER() [1/2]

iRRAM::WIENER::WIENER  | ( | | ) |   
---|---|---|---|---  
  
Generate the sample path in probability.

Generate the sample path computed with probability 1-2^(-50) using Levy-
ciesielski construction. See [ Mörters, P., & Peres, Y. (2010). Brownian
motion (Vol. 30) ]

## ◆ WIENER() [2/2]

iRRAM::WIENER::WIENER  | ( | int | _p_| ) |   
---|---|---|---|---|---  
  
Generate the sample path in probability.

Generate the sample path computed with probability 1-2^(-p) using Levy-
ciesielski construction. See [ Mörters, P., & Peres, Y. (2010). Brownian
motion (Vol. 30) ]

##  Modules  
  
---  
| [Level 1](group__linalg_sub1.html)  
| operations that do not require any computation on matrix elements; e.g.,
swaping rows  
  
| [Level 2](group__linalg_sub2.html)  
| operations that require simple computations on matrix elements; e.g., matrix
norms  
  
| [Level 3](group__linalg_sub3.html)  
| operations that require iteration over rows and columns; e.g., computing
determinant  
  
| [Level 4](group__linalg_sub4.html)  
| operations operations that requiare complicated stuffs..; e.g., eigenproblem

| ) | |   
  
create a matrix from given submatrices

If A is of dimension n * m, B should be of dimension n * k for some k. And, C
should be of dimension of l * m for some l and D should be of dimension l * k

Returns

    a matrix which is [A B ; C D]

## ◆ givens()

REALMATRIX iRRAM::givens  | ( | unsigned int | _n_ ,   
---|---|---|---  
|  | unsigned int | _i_ ,   
|  | unsigned int | _j_ ,   
|  | REAL | _c_ ,   
|  | REAL | _s_  
| ) | |   
  
create Given's rotation

return the Given's n-dimentional rotation G where G(i,i) = G(j,j) = c and
G(j,i) = s = - G(i, j)

Parameters

     n| the dimension of Given's rotation   
---|---  
i| 0 <= a row index < n  
j| 0 <= a column index < n  
  
Warning

    r^2 + c^2 should be 1

Returns

    a matrix which is [A B ; C D]

## Function Documentation

## ◆ hessenberg_reduction()

REALMATRIX iRRAM::hessenberg_reduction  | ( | REALMATRIX | _M_ ,   
---|---|---|---  
|  | int | _p_  
| ) | |   
  
approximately reduce a square matrix to Hessenberg matrix

For a square matrix M, compute a Hessenberg matrix H. It holds that the
eigenvalues of H are perturbed from the eigenvalues of M by at most 2^p

Parameters

     M| a square matrix   
---|---  
p| negative integer  
  
Returns

    a Hessenberg matrix whose eigenvalues approximates the eigenvalues of M

## ◆ haar_orthogonal_matrix()

REALMATRIX iRRAM::haar_orthogonal_matrix  | ( | unsigned int | _n_| ) |   
---|---|---|---|---|---  
  
Gaussian regular Matrix sampler.

returns uniformly distributed orthogonal matrix (that follows Haar measure in
O(n)). See [Stewart, Gilbert W. "The efficient generation of random orthogonal
matrices with an application to condition estimators." SIAM Journal on
Numerical Analysis 17.3 (1980): 403-409.] for more detail.
